# WP17 mission-exit baseline (NFR-001 / SC-009), mission
# review-cycle-verdict-seam-rebuild-01KZ2W7W.
# The FULL collected node-id set (2877 entries) for the affected-suites
# invocation named in quickstart.md's "Before anything: the baseline"
# section and research/baseline-8466727eb.md's "Invocation" section:
#
#   PWHEADLESS=1 uv run pytest \
#     tests/review/ tests/status/ \
#     tests/regression/test_2646_stale_verdict_closes_via_fr001.py \
#     tests/integration/test_review_cycle_rejection_only.py \
#     tests/integration/test_ac5_hash_guard.py \
#     tests/integration/test_wp_file_hash_stability.py \
#     tests/post_merge/test_review_artifact_consistency.py \
#     tests/specify_cli/cli/commands/agent/ -q
#
# This is the NFR-001 FLOOR: it may grow (new tests are welcome), it may
# never shrink (a committed node-id vanishing -- deleted, moved out of the
# affected-suites paths, de-parametrized, or excluded at collection/marker
# level -- is a violation, not an improvement). Enforced mechanically by
# tests/architectural/test_mission_exit_baseline.py. Regenerate only when
# the affected-suites invocation itself legitimately changes scope, with a
# comment recorded in the updated research/baseline-8466727eb.md section
# explaining why -- never silently.
#
# Measured 2026-08-05 at mission exit: 2874 passed, 1 skipped, 2 xfailed,
# 0 failed (2877 collected). Both node-ids the pre-mission baseline
# (research/baseline-8466727eb.md, measured at merge-base 8466727eb) named
# as failing -- #3157 and #3160 -- now PASS; see that document's dated
# mission-exit section for the reconciliation.
#
# 2026-08-05 REBASE RE-ANCHOR: regenerated against upstream/main after the
# mission was rebased onto it. Collected count 2886 (was 2877 at 8466727eb);
# the delta reflects main's own test additions/relocations (e.g. the #2996
# reproductions now live in tests/regression/test_issue_2996_*.py). Floor
# re-anchored to current main per NFR-001's merge-base rule.
#
# 2026-08-06 TARGETED RENAME SWAP (PR #3209 landing pass, one line, NOT a
# regeneration): the single node-id
#   tests/status/test_reducer.py::TestEventSourcedReviewResultReader::
#     test_event_sourced_review_result_this_missions_own_meta_json_fixture
# was replaced by
#   tests/status/test_reducer.py::TestEventSourcedReviewResultReader::
#     test_event_sourced_review_result_coord_primary_partition_slot_absent
# in commit 42f0a1ed5 (a #3220 fold). The old test used this repository's own
# live kitty-specs/review-cycle-verdict-seam-rebuild-01KZ2W7W/meta.json as its
# fixture and asserted that file lacked a status_phase key; that mission was
# later migrated and gained the key, so the live precondition silently stopped
# holding and the test went red for a reason unrelated to the reader it covers.
# The replacement builds the same coord-topology primary-partition shape
# synthetically under tmp_path, so it is true by construction rather than only
# at the instant it was written. Coverage of the reader is preserved, not
# reduced: this is a rename, not a deletion, de-parametrization, or exclusion.
#
# Verified before committing this swap, against the EXACT affected-suites
# invocation quoted above (uv run pytest --collect-only -q -p no:cacheprovider):
# 2890 node-ids collected; the new node-id is present in that live collection by
# name, and the floor-minus-live difference was EXACTLY the one old node-id
# above and nothing else. The floor also grew 2886 -> 2890 (growth is permitted).
# The invocation's own scope is unchanged, so the "regenerate when the
# invocation legitimately changes scope" path above deliberately does NOT apply
# and the file was NOT regenerated -- a wholesale regeneration here would have
# laundered any genuine concurrent selection regression into green.
